Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the approximation framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 915. The resulting operator achieves bound O(816/n^3).

This completes the proof. QED.
